{"forms":[{"form":"fold equities","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Alice holds A♣6♥. Brian holds 2♥2♦. Flop: 9♠7♣3♦. If there's no more betting, the pot equity at showdown is: Alice = 31.5%, Brian = 68.5%. But, if Alice bets and Brian is 70% likely to fold, then Alice's fold equity is 70% × 68.5% = 48.2925%. Total pot equity if Alice bets = 31.5% + 48.2925% = 79.7925%.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The portion of the pot one expects to win, on average, by a bet that induces opponents to fold, rather than by seeing the showdown."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["card-games","poker"]}],"upstream_line":6125659,"upstream_offset":13791075231,"upstream_record_sha256":"9f296c6474df5ff085e14514bb2db272fd78a2909a64fb3a607643721ed9c9a4","word":"fold equity"}
